# List 15

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>liberal</b></td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonconventional</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnkənˈvɛnʃənəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常规的，不合传统的</td><td>nonconventional energy sources<br>非常规能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city invested in nonconventional energy such as tidal power.  该市投资了潮汐能等非常规能源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonorthodox</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈɔrθəˌdɑks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非正统的</td><td>a nonorthodox reading<br>非正统的解读</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She offered a nonorthodox reading of the ancient poem.  她对这首古诗提出了非正统的解读。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nontraditional</td><td>/ˌnɑːntrəˈdɪʃənəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非传统的，不循常规的</td><td>a nontraditional approach<br>非传统的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school welcomes nontraditional students who return after working.  这所学校欢迎工作后返校的非传统学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 open-minded</td><td>/ˈoʊpən ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想开明的</td><td>an open-minded editor<br>思想开明的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good reviewer stays open-minded about unfamiliar musical styles.  好的评论者对陌生的音乐风格保持开明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 progressive</td><td>/prəˈɡresɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>进步的，开明的</td><td>progressive social policies<br>进步的社会政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school follows progressive methods that welcome student questions.  这所学校采用鼓励学生提问的进步方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 radical</td><td>/ˈrædəkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>激进的；根本的；激进者</td><td>radical reform<br>激进的改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council rejected any radical change to the old charter.  议会否决了对旧章程的任何激进改动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 munificent</td><td>/mjuːˈnɪfɪsnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨的</td><td>a munificent donation<br>一笔慷慨的捐赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university received a munificent donation from a former engineering student.  这所大学收到了一位工程系校友的慷慨捐赠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unselfish</td><td>/ʌnˈselfɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无私的，不自私的</td><td>unselfish devotion<br>无私的奉献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unselfish passing made the whole team better.  他无私的传球让整支球队更出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsparing</td><td>/ʌnˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不留情的；毫不吝惜的</td><td>unsparing criticism<br>毫不留情的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review was unsparing about the actor's weak performance.  这篇评论对该演员糟糕的表演毫不留情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unstinting</td><td>/ʌnˈstɪntɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不吝惜的，大方的</td><td>unstinting support<br>毫不吝惜的支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave unstinting support to every young researcher.  她对每位年轻研究者都给予毫不吝惜的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conservative</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，不愿改变的；不招摇的，低调的</td><td>a conservative estimate<br>保守的估计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were too conservative to accept such an unconventional career choice.  她的父母太守旧，无法接受这样不合常规的职业选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conventional</td><td>/kənˈvenʃənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>传统的，常规的</td><td>conventional wisdom<br>传统观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They chose a conventional design to please the older members.  他们选了常规设计以取悦年长的成员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hidebound</td><td>/ˈhaɪdbaʊnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>死板的，极度保守的</td><td>hidebound traditions<br>僵化的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hidebound innkeeper flatly refused to install internet for guests.  这位死板的旅店老板断然拒绝为客人装网络。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 old-fashioned</td><td>/ˌoʊldˈfæʃənd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，过时的</td><td>old-fashioned manners<br>守旧的礼节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His old-fashioned views on marriage puzzled his own children.  他守旧的婚姻观让自己的孩子困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stodgy</td><td>/ˈstɑːdʒi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古板守旧的，乏味的</td><td>a stodgy old textbook<br>一本乏味的旧教材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The course relied on a stodgy old textbook nobody enjoyed reading.  这门课用的是一本没人爱读的乏味旧教材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 traditional</td><td>/trəˈdɪʃənəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>传统的，守旧的</td><td>traditional values<br>传统价值观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parents hold rather traditional views on marriage.  他父母对婚姻持相当传统的看法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 closefisted</del></td><td>/ˌkloʊsˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，一毛不拔的</td><td>a closefisted landlord<br>吝啬的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their closefisted landlord refused to fix the heater.  他们吝啬的房东拒绝修暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 miserly</td><td>/ˈmaɪzərli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a miserly tip<br>吝啬的小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He left a miserly tip after complaining about the service all evening.  他整晚抱怨服务，最后只留下吝啬的小费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 niggardly</td><td>/ˈnɪɡərdli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，少得可怜的</td><td>a niggardly raise<br>少得可怜的加薪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered a niggardly raise after record profits.  在创纪录盈利后公司给了少得可怜的加薪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 parsimonious</td><td>/ˌpɑːrsəˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，过分节俭的</td><td>parsimonious with praise<br>极少给出赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parsimonious landlord refused to replace the broken heater.  吝啬的房东拒绝更换坏了的暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stingy</td><td>/ˈstɪndʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>小气的，吝啬的</td><td>stingy with money<br>在钱上很吝啬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is stingy with money but surprisingly generous with his time.  他在钱上吝啬，在时间上却出奇大方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 tightfisted</del></td><td>/ˌtaɪtˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a tightfisted employer<br>吝啬的雇主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their tightfisted employer counted every paper clip.  他们吝啬的雇主连回形针都要数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>libertine</b></td><td>/ˈlɪbərtiːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>放荡不羁者</td><td>an unrepentant libertine<br>不知悔改的放荡者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play portrays an unrepentant libertine who ruins everyone around him.  这部戏刻画了一个毁掉身边所有人的不知悔改的放荡者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 backslider</del></td><td>/ˈbækslaɪdər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落者，故态复萌者</td><td>a hopeless backslider<br>无望改好的堕落者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The preacher warned the congregation against becoming backsliders.  牧师警告信众不要故态复萌堕落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 debaucher</del></td><td>/dɪˈbɔːtʃər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>放荡者，使人堕落者</td><td>a notorious debaucher<br>声名狼藉的放荡者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The diary paints the duke as a shameless debaucher.  那本日记把公爵描绘成无耻的放荡者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decadent</td><td>/ˈdekədənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，腐败的</td><td>a decadent ruling class<br>腐败堕落的统治阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the court as decadent, idle, and blind to reform.  他形容那个宫廷腐败堕落、无所作为、对改革视而不见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deviate</td><td>/ˈdiːviˌeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>偏离，背离</td><td>deviate from the plan<br>偏离计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot had to deviate from the planned route because of storms.  因风暴，飞行员不得不偏离既定航线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pervert</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>曲解，使颠倒；使堕落</td><td>pervert the course of justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawyers accused him of trying to pervert the truth in court.  律师指控他试图在法庭上曲解真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profligate</td><td>/ˈprɑːflɪɡət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>挥金如土的，挥霍的</td><td>profligate spending<br>挥霍无度的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council's profligate spending on ceremonies angered local taxpayers.  议会在典礼上挥霍无度的开支激怒了当地纳税人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ascetic</td><td>/əˈsetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>苦行的，禁欲的；苦行者</td><td>an ascetic lifestyle<br>苦修的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk led an ascetic life on bread and cold water.  这位僧人以面包和冷水过着自我克制的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>licentious</b></td><td>/laɪˈsenʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>放荡的；淫乱的</td><td>licentious behavior<br>放荡的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His licentious behavior at the party shocked even his closest friends.  他在派对上放荡的行为连最亲近的朋友都感到震惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 concupiscent</del></td><td>/kɑːnˈkjuːpɪsənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好色的，纵欲的</td><td>concupiscent desires<br>纵欲的欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The satire mocks the concupiscent courtiers of the era.  这部讽刺作品嘲弄那个时代好色的朝臣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 horny</td><td>/ˈhɔːrni/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好色的，性欲强的</td><td>get horny<br>起了色心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is mostly about horny teenagers on holiday.  这部电影主要讲度假中好色的少年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lascivious</td><td>/ləˈsɪviəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好色的，淫荡的</td><td>a lascivious glance<br>淫邪的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was fired for lascivious comments to coworkers.  他因对同事说淫秽言语被解雇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lecherous</td><td>/ˈletʃərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好色的，淫荡的</td><td>a lecherous old man<br>好色的老头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's lecherous villain is oddly comic.  小说中好色的反派意外地滑稽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 libidinous</td><td>/lɪˈbɪdɪnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好色的，纵欲的</td><td>libidinous impulses<br>纵欲的冲动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the poem libidinous and unpublishable.  评论家称这首诗淫秽且不宜出版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lubricious</del></td><td>/luˈbrɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淫荡的，好色的</td><td>lubricious jokes<br>下流的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lubricious jokes emptied the table quickly.  他下流的玩笑很快让桌边的人走光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salacious</td><td>/səˈleɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>猥亵的，色情的</td><td>salacious gossip<br>低俗色情的传闻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tabloid printed salacious details about the trial.  小报刊登了关于审判的色情细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wanton</td><td>/ˈwɔːntən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>放荡的；恣意的</td><td>wanton destruction<br>恣意的破坏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court condemned the wanton damage to the church.  法庭谴责对教堂的恣意破坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frigid</td><td>/ˈfrɪdʒɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严寒的；冷漠的</td><td>a frigid stare<br>冷冰冰的凝视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the latecomer a frigid stare and said nothing.  她冷冰冰地盯了迟到者一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 undersexed</del></td><td>/ˌʌndərˈsekst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>性欲低下的</td><td>an undersexed marriage<br>缺少性生活的婚姻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's hero is lonely, undersexed, and oddly cheerful.  小说主角孤独、性欲淡薄，却出奇地开朗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>liken</b></td><td>/ˈlaɪkən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…比作，比拟</td><td>liken life to a journey<br>把人生比作旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poets often liken life to a long and uncertain journey.  诗人常把生活比作一场漫长而不确定的旅程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 analogize</del></td><td>/əˈnælədʒaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>比拟，类推</td><td>analogize two cases<br>把两个案例作类比</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawyers analogize this dispute to an earlier tax case.  律师把这场争议类比为早前的税务案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bracket</td><td>/ˈbrækɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…归为一类，并列</td><td>bracket the two writers<br>把这两位作家归为一类</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics wrongly bracket her with the surrealists.  评论家错误地把她与超现实主义者归为一类。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equate</td><td>/ɪˈkweɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…等同看待</td><td>equate wealth with success<br>把财富等同于成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not equate silence with agreement.  不要把沉默等同于同意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equalize</td><td>/ˈiːkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使相等，使平等</td><td>equalize opportunities<br>使机会平等</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform aims to equalize funding across districts.  这项改革旨在使各区经费均等。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contrast</td><td>/ˈkɑːntræst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>差异，对比；对照</td><td>a sharp contrast<br>鲜明的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a sharp contrast between his words and his actions.  他的言语与行为之间有鲜明的差异。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>limber</b></td><td>/ˈlɪmbər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软灵活的</td><td>stay limber and loose<br>保持柔软放松</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dancers stretch every morning to stay limber and avoid painful injuries.  舞者每天早上拉伸，以保持柔软并避免疼痛的伤病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lissome</del></td><td>/ˈlɪsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软的；敏捷的，轻盈的</td><td>a lissome dancer<br>身姿柔软的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lissome dancer moved across the stage as if she were weightless.  那位身姿柔软的舞者仿佛毫无重量地掠过舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lithesome</del></td><td>/ˈlaɪðsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软的，轻盈敏捷的</td><td>lithesome movements<br>轻盈的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lithesome gymnast crossed the beam without a wobble.  这位轻盈的体操运动员走过平衡木毫无摇晃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supple</td><td>/ˈsʌpl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的</td><td>supple leather<br>柔软的皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloves were made of supple leather that fit her hands perfectly.  这双手套用柔软的皮革制成，和她的手完全贴合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflexible</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfleksəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不肯变通的，僵化的</td><td>an inflexible rule<br>不容变通的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was inflexible about the submission deadline.  经理在提交截止日期上毫不通融。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rigid</td><td>/ˈrɪdʒəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严格的，僵硬的</td><td>rigid discipline<br>严格的纪律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school enforces rigid rules about mobile phones.  学校对手机执行严格的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiff</td><td>/stɪf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵硬的，无法弯曲的；艰苦的，费力的</td><td>a stiff leather collar<br>硬挺的皮质衣领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff leather collar rubbed his neck raw during the long march.  长途行军中，硬挺的皮领把他的脖子磨破了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stiffened</td><td>/ˈstɪfənd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>变硬的，僵硬的</td><td>stiffened joints<br>僵硬的关节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stiffened fingers could barely grip the pen.  他僵硬的手指几乎握不住笔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>limp</b></td><td>/lɪmp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、形容词</td><td>跛行，艰难缓慢地走；柔软的</td><td>limp toward the door<br>跛行走向门口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He limped slowly toward the door with his ankle wrapped in bandages.  他脚踝缠着绷带，一步一跛地慢慢走向门口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 droopy</td><td>/ˈdruːpi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>下垂的，松垂的</td><td>droopy eyelids<br>下垂的眼皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog's droopy ears made it look permanently sad.  这只狗下垂的耳朵让它看起来总是很忧伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaccid</td><td>/ˈflæsɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不结实的，松弛的</td><td>flaccid muscles<br>松弛的肌肉</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 floppy</td><td>/ˈflɑːpi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>松软的，松垂的</td><td>a floppy hat<br>软塌塌的帽子</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lank</td><td>/læŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>细长瘦弱的；不僵硬的，柔软松弛的</td><td>lank hair<br>柔软下垂的头发</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blunder</td><td>/ˈblʌndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>大错，失误；犯大错</td><td>a costly blunder<br>代价高昂的失误</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 trudge</td><td>/trʌdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>吃力而笨拙地走</td><td>trudge through the snow<br>在雪中吃力行走</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 cloudy</td><td>/ˈklaʊdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>混浊的；多云的</td><td>cloudy liquid<br>混浊的液体</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 opaque</td><td>/oʊˈpeɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不透明的；晦涩难懂的</td><td>opaque legal language<br>晦涩的法律用语</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the storm the once clear stream turned brown and turbid.  暴风雨过后，原本清澈的溪水变得棕黄混浊。</td></tr>

<tr><td><b>linger</b></td><td>/ˈlɪŋɡər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>磨蹭，闲荡</td><td>linger in the doorway<br>在门口徘徊磨蹭</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 hurry</td><td>/ˈhɜːri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙前行，赶紧</td><td>hurry along the corridor<br>匆忙穿过走廊</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 run</td><td>/rʌn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>跑，奔跑；运转</td><td>run for the bus<br>跑着赶公交</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran three miles before breakfast every single morning.  他每天早餐前都跑三英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rush</td><td>/rʌʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙，急速行动；催促</td><td>rush to the airport<br>赶往机场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed to the airport and barely caught the flight.  他们赶往机场，勉强赶上了航班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>lissome</b></del></td><td>/ˈlɪsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软的；敏捷的，轻盈的</td><td>a lissome dancer<br>身姿柔软的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lissome dancer moved across the stage as if she were weightless.  那位身姿柔软的舞者仿佛毫无重量地掠过舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limber</td><td>/ˈlɪmbər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软灵活的</td><td>stay limber and loose<br>保持柔软放松</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dancers stretch every morning to stay limber and avoid painful injuries.  舞者每天早上拉伸，以保持柔软并避免疼痛的伤病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lithe</td><td>/laɪð/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，轻盈的；柔软的</td><td>a lithe young cat<br>轻盈的小猫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was lithe enough to slip through the narrow gap.  她身形轻盈，足以从窄缝中溜过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supple</td><td>/ˈsʌpl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的</td><td>supple leather<br>柔软的皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gloves were made of supple leather that fit her hands perfectly.  这双手套用柔软的皮革制成，和她的手完全贴合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agile</td><td>/ˈædʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，灵活的</td><td>an agile climber<br>敏捷的攀登者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agile goat crossed the rocks without slipping.  那只敏捷的山羊越过岩石毫不滑倒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 featly</del></td><td>/ˈfiːtli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>副词</td><td>轻盈灵巧地</td><td>dance featly<br>轻盈地舞动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancers moved featly across the wooden stage.  舞者轻盈地在木质舞台上移动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feline</td><td>/ˈfiːˌlaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>猫科的；像猫般轻盈的</td><td>feline grace<br>猫一般的优雅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She turned with a feline grace that silenced the room.  她以猫一般的优雅转身，让全屋安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gracile</td><td>/ˈɡræsəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>纤细优雅的，苗条的</td><td>a gracile figure<br>纤细优雅的身形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statue shows a gracile youth holding a lamp.  这尊雕像刻的是持灯的纤瘦少年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 lithesome</del></td><td>/ˈlaɪðsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软的，轻盈敏捷的</td><td>lithesome movements<br>轻盈的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lithesome gymnast crossed the beam without a wobble.  这位轻盈的体操运动员走过平衡木毫无摇晃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nimble</td><td>/ˈnɪmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏捷的，灵活的</td><td>nimble fingers<br>灵活的手指</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her nimble fingers tied the knot in one second.  她灵活的手指一秒钟就打好了结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 solid</td><td>/ˈsɑːlɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗壮结实的</td><td>a solid, heavy build<br>粗壮结实的体格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wrestler had a solid, heavy build unlike his slender opponent.  这名摔跤手体格粗壮结实，与瘦削的对手截然不同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 awkward</td><td>/ˈɔːkwərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的；尴尬的</td><td>an awkward silence<br>尴尬的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An awkward silence followed his remark about her divorce.  他谈到她离婚之后，出现了一阵尴尬的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clumsy</td><td>/ˈklʌmzi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的</td><td>a clumsy attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My clumsy attempt at juggling ended with three broken plates.  我笨拙地学抛球，结果打碎了三个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 graceless</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不优雅的</td><td>a graceless heavy stride<br>笨重不雅的步态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His graceless attempt at an apology made things worse.  他笨拙的道歉尝试让事情更糟了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ungainly</td><td>/ʌnˈɡeɪnli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不雅的</td><td>an ungainly walk<br>笨拙的步态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young bird made an ungainly landing on the narrow branch.  那只幼鸟笨拙地落在窄窄的树枝上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>list</b></td><td>/lɪst/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）倾斜</td><td>the ship began to list<br>船开始倾斜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The overloaded ship began to list badly in the rising waves.  这艘超载的船在渐高的浪中开始严重倾斜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 angle</td><td>/ˈæŋɡəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使倾斜；角度</td><td>angle the mirror<br>把镜子调斜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He angled the lamp so the light fell on the page.  他把灯调斜，使光照在书页上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cant</td><td>/ˈkænt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>行话；伪善之言</td><td>political cant<br>政治套话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was pure cant dressed up as principle.  他的演讲不过是伪装成原则的套话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heel</td><td>/ˈhiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（船）倾斜；脚跟</td><td>heel over in the wind<br>在风中倾斜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The yacht heeled sharply as the gust hit.  阵风袭来时游艇急剧倾斜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incline</td><td>/ɪnˈklaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）倾斜；倾向</td><td>incline one's head<br>把头倾向一侧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She inclined her head slightly to hear him.  她微微侧头以便听清他说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pitch</td><td>/ˈpɪtʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（船、飞机）颠簸倾斜</td><td>pitch in heavy seas<br>在大浪中颠簸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boat pitched so badly that dishes slid off.  船颠簸得厉害，盘子都滑落了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slant</td><td>/slænt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）倾斜；斜度</td><td>the floor slants slightly<br>地面略微倾斜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old wooden floor slants slightly toward the far corner.  这块旧木地板向远处的角落略微倾斜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slope</td><td>/ˈsloʊp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>倾斜；斜坡</td><td>slope gently downward<br>缓缓向下倾斜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawn slopes gently toward the river.  草坪缓缓向河边倾斜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tilt</td><td>/tɪlt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）倾斜；倾斜度</td><td>tilt the screen back<br>把屏幕向后倾斜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tilted the screen back until the glare finally disappeared.  他把屏幕向后倾斜，直到反光终于消失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tip</td><td>/ˈtɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）倾斜，倾倒</td><td>tip the chair back<br>把椅子向后倾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not tip the glass or the tea will spill.  别把杯子倾斜，茶会洒出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 erect</td><td>/ɪˈrekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>直立的，垂直的</td><td>stand erect<br>笔直地站着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old soldier still stood perfectly erect.  这位老兵仍站得笔直。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disinclined</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈklaɪnd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的，不情愿的</td><td>disinclined to help<br>不愿帮忙</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indisposed</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愿的；身体不适的</td><td>indisposed to talk<br>不愿交谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed indisposed to talk about that evening.  证人似乎不愿谈那晚的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reluctant</td><td>/rɪˈlʌktənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不情愿的，勉强的</td><td>reluctant to answer<br>不愿回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed reluctant to answer any question about that evening.  证人似乎不愿回答关于那天晚上的任何问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reticent</td><td>/ˈretɪsnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默不语的</td><td>reticent about his past<br>对往事讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was oddly reticent about the years he spent working abroad.  他对自己在国外工作的那些年出奇地沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eager</td><td>/ˈiːɡər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>渴望的，热切的</td><td>eager to learn<br>渴望学习</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were eager to open their presents at once.  孩子们急切地想立刻打开礼物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>loathe</b></td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abhor</td><td>/əbˈhɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极度厌恶，深恶痛绝</td><td>abhor violence<br>痛恨暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abhors any form of cruelty to animals.  她极度厌恶任何形式的虐待动物行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abominate</del></td><td>/əˈbɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，厌恶</td><td>abominate cruelty<br>憎恶残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abominated the hypocrisy he saw all around him.  他痛恨周围随处可见的虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despise</td><td>/dɪˈspaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鄙视，极其不喜欢</td><td>despise a coward<br>鄙视懦夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She despised him for abandoning his closest friends.  她鄙视他抛弃至交的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detest</td><td>/dɪˈtest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>厌恶，憎恨</td><td>detest hypocrisy<br>厌恶虚伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I detest waiting in long lines on hot days.  我讨厌在炎热的日子里排长队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 execrate</del></td><td>/ˈeksɪkreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，咒骂</td><td>execrate a tyrant<br>痛斥暴君</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still execrate the tyrant for his cruelty.  史学家仍因其残暴而痛斥这个暴君。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adore</td><td>/əˈdɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>热爱，极喜爱；崇拜</td><td>absolutely adore someone<br>非常喜爱某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children adore their new puppy.  孩子们非常喜爱他们的新小狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 love</td><td>/ˈlʌv/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>爱，热爱；喜爱</td><td>fall in love with<br>爱上，与…相爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I love walking along the river in the early morning.  我喜欢清晨沿着河边散步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lofty</b></td><td>/ˈlɔːfti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；自大的</td><td>a lofty tone<br>自大的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dismissed our idea with a lofty wave of her hand.  她自大地一挥手就否定了我们的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chivalrous</td><td>/ˈʃɪvəlrəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有骑士风度的，（尤指对女性）彬彬有礼的</td><td>a chivalrous gesture<br>有骑士风度的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made a chivalrous offer to carry her heavy suitcase.  他很有骑士风度地提出替她拿沉重的行李箱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elevated</td><td>/ˈeləˌveɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高尚的，崇高的；升高的</td><td>elevated language<br>高雅的语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech used elevated language that moved the crowd.  这篇演讲用高雅的语言感动了人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 greathearted</del></td><td>/ˌɡreɪtˈhɑːrtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽宏大量的，勇敢的</td><td>a greathearted friend<br>宽宏大量的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her greathearted forgiveness surprised even her critics.  她宽宏的原谅甚至让批评者惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 high-minded</td><td>/ˌhaɪˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>品格高尚的，有原则的</td><td>high-minded principles<br>高尚的原则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His high-minded refusal of the bribe cost him the job.  他高尚地拒绝贿赂，因此失去了工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnanimous</td><td>/mæɡˈnænəməs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽宏大量的，大度的</td><td>a magnanimous concession<br>大度的让步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was magnanimous in victory and praised her rival.  她胜而不骄，还称赞了对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sublime</td><td>/səˈblaɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；壮丽的；卓越的</td><td>a sublime landscape<br>崇高壮丽的景色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the summit we watched a sublime landscape unfold below us.  从山顶我们看着一片壮丽的景色在下方展开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 assumptive</del></td><td>/əˈsʌmptɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，自以为是的；假定的</td><td>an assumptive attitude<br>傲慢的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His assumptive manner annoyed everyone at the negotiating table.  他傲慢的态度让谈判桌上的人都很反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bumptious</td><td>/ˈbʌmpʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横傲慢的，自以为是的</td><td>a bumptious young man<br>自以为是的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her bumptious manner annoyed the older members of the committee.  她自以为是的态度让委员会里年长的成员很反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haughty</td><td>/ˈhɔːti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的，目中无人的</td><td>a haughty manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave the waiter a haughty look and said nothing.  她傲慢地看了服务员一眼，什么也没说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lordly</td><td>/ˈlɔːrdli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>傲慢的；高贵的</td><td>a lordly manner<br>傲慢的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He waved the waiter away with a lordly gesture.  他以傲慢的手势把侍者挥退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peremptory</td><td>/pəˈremptəri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不容置疑的；专横傲慢的</td><td>a peremptory order<br>不容置疑的命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His peremptory tone left no room for questions or discussion.  他傲慢的语气不给任何提问或讨论的余地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pompous</td><td>/ˈpɑːmpəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>浮夸的；傲慢的</td><td>a pompous speech<br>浮夸的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pompous speech bored everyone in the crowded town hall.  那场浮夸的演讲让拥挤的市政厅里每个人都厌烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumptuous</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmptʃuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冒失的，放肆的</td><td>presumptuous of you<br>你太放肆了</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seems presumptuous to criticize a book you have not read.  批评一本没读过的书，似乎太放肆了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supercilious</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈsɪliəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高傲的，傲慢的</td><td>a supercilious smile<br>傲慢的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A supercilious smile crossed his face when he heard my question.  听到我的问题，他脸上掠过傲慢的微笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superior</td><td>/suːˈpɪriər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>更好的，占优势的；上级</td><td>superior to the rest<br>优于其余各方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This model is clearly superior to the older version.  这个型号明显优于旧版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 base</td><td>/ˈbeɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑劣的，低下的</td><td>base motives<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He acted from base motives of greed and envy.  他出于贪婪和嫉妒的卑劣动机行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debased</td><td>/dəˈbeɪst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>堕落的，贬损的</td><td>debased taste<br>低劣的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He complained about the debased state of public debate.  他抱怨公共辩论的堕落状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignominious</td><td>/ˌɪɡnəˈmɪniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可耻的，不光彩的</td><td>an ignominious defeat<br>可耻的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The season ended in ignominious defeat at home.  这个赛季以主场可耻的失利告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mean</td><td>/miːn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的；吝啬的</td><td>a mean little room<br>简陋卑贱的小屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family lived in a mean little room above a noisy printing shop.  一家人住在嘈杂印刷店楼上一间简陋的小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lowly</td><td>/ˈloʊli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>地位低的，卑微的</td><td>a lowly clerk<br>一个卑微的职员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He began as a lowly assistant and rose to direct the firm.  他从卑微的助理做起，后来掌管了公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>loll</b></td><td>/lɑːl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>懒躺，闲混度日</td><td>loll on the sofa<br>懒躺在沙发上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children lolled on the sofa instead of finishing their homework.  孩子们懒躺在沙发上，而不是把作业做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dally</td><td>/ˈdæli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>虚度时光，磨蹭</td><td>dally over lunch<br>吃午饭磨蹭半天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't dally on the way home; dinner is almost ready.  回家路上别磨蹭，晚饭快好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dawdle</td><td>/ˈdɔːdl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拖拖拉拉，磨蹭</td><td>dawdle on the way<br>路上拖拖拉拉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dawdled over her homework until it was nearly midnight.  她做作业拖拖拉拉，一直到近午夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drone</td><td>/droʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>低沉单调地说；嗡嗡地叫</td><td>drone on about details<br>唠唠叨叨讲细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker droned on for another twenty minutes.  演讲者又单调地讲了二十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laze</td><td>/leɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>懒散度日，闲躺</td><td>laze in the sun<br>在阳光下懒躺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lazed by the pool all Sunday afternoon.  他们整个周日下午都在池边懒躺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lopsided</b></td><td>/ˌlɑːpˈsaɪdɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平衡的，不协调的</td><td>a lopsided victory<br>一场悬殊的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final score revealed a lopsided victory for the visiting team.  最终比分显示客队取得了一场悬殊的胜利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 asymmetric</td><td>/ˌeɪsəˈmetrɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不对称的，不均衡的</td><td>asymmetric warfare<br>非对称战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides fought an asymmetric war for over a decade.  双方进行了十多年的非对称战争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disproportional</td><td>/ˌdɪsprəˈpɔːrʃənəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不成比例的，不匀称的</td><td>a disproportional share<br>不成比例的份额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small towns bear a disproportional share of the cost.  小城镇承担了不成比例的成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irregular</td><td>/ɪˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不规则的，不平整的</td><td>an irregular shape<br>不规则的形状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path was made of irregular stones of many sizes.  这条小路由各种大小不规则的石头铺成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 off-balance</td><td>/ˌɔːf ˈbæləns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>失去平衡的</td><td>knock someone off-balance<br>把某人撞得失去平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden question caught the speaker off-balance.  突然的提问让演讲者措手不及。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbalanced</td><td>/ʌnˈbælənst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平衡的；失衡的</td><td>an unbalanced diet<br>不均衡的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report gave an unbalanced picture of the city's economy.  这份报告对该市经济的描述有失均衡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unequal</td><td>/ʌnˈiːkwəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相等的，不平等的</td><td>unequal treatment<br>不平等的待遇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides entered the contest on decidedly unequal terms.  双方是在明显不平等的条件下参赛的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 balanced</td><td>/ˈbælənst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>均衡的，协调的</td><td>a balanced diet<br>均衡的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room felt calm because the colors were carefully balanced.  房间给人平静感，因为色彩经过精心搭配。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>loquacious</b></td><td>/ləˈkweɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>话多的</td><td>a loquacious guide<br>话多的导游</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our loquacious guide talked steadily from the museum door to the exit.  我们话多的导游从博物馆门口一直讲到出口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chatty</td><td>/ˈtʃæti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，爱聊天的</td><td>a chatty neighbor<br>健谈的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our chatty neighbor kept us at the door for an hour.  健谈的邻居让我们在门口站了一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conversational</td><td>/ˌkɑːnvərˈseɪʃənəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，好交谈的</td><td>a conversational host<br>健谈的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our conversational host kept every guest talking until well past midnight.  我们健谈的主人让每位客人一直聊到午夜之后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gabby</td><td>/ˈɡæbi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>话多的，唠叨的</td><td>a gabby neighbor<br>话多的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our gabby neighbor kept us at the gate for an hour.  话多的邻居把我们留在门口一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garrulous</td><td>/ˈɡærələs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>啰嗦的，话多令人厌烦的</td><td>a garrulous neighbor<br>唠叨的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our garrulous neighbor talked for an hour about his lawn.  我们唠叨的邻居就他的草坪讲了一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 talkative</td><td>/ˈtɔːkətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，爱说话的</td><td>a talkative passenger<br>健谈的乘客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our talkative driver described every building along the winding coastal road.  健谈的司机介绍了蜿蜒海滨路上的每座建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 voluble</td><td>/ˈvɑːljəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，话多的</td><td>a voluble speaker<br>健谈的讲话者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our voluble guide talked cheerfully about every stone in the old city.  我们健谈的导游兴致勃勃地讲述古城里的每一块石头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 laconic</td><td>/ləˈkɑːnɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁（以至于显得粗鲁或难以理解）的</td><td>a laconic reply<br>简短生硬的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His laconic reply told us nothing about what had actually happened.  他简短生硬的回答没有告诉我们实际发生了什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reserved</td><td>/rɪˈzɜːrvd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向的，缄默的</td><td>a quiet, reserved man<br>安静内向的男子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her father was a reserved man who rarely spoke about his feelings.  她的父亲是个内向的人，很少谈论自己的感受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reticent</td><td>/ˈretɪsnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默不语的</td><td>reticent about his past<br>对往事讳莫如深</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was oddly reticent about the years he spent working abroad.  他对自己在国外工作的那些年出奇地沉默不语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 taciturn</td><td>/ˈtæsɪtɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，话少的</td><td>a taciturn farmer<br>沉默寡言的农夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The taciturn farmer answered our questions with a nod or a shrug.  沉默寡言的农夫只用点头或耸肩回答我们的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncommunicative</td><td>/ˌʌnkəˈmjuːnɪkətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，不爱说话的</td><td>an uncommunicative teenager<br>沉默寡言的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew uncommunicative after the move to a new school.  转到新学校后他变得沉默寡言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>loutish</b></td><td>/ˈlaʊtɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的</td><td>loutish manners<br>粗鲁的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His loutish manners at the dinner table embarrassed his entire family.  他在餐桌上粗鲁的举止让全家人都很难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boorish</td><td>/ˈbʊrɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁无礼的</td><td>boorish behavior<br>粗鲁无礼的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His boorish behavior at the table embarrassed everyone present.  他在餐桌上粗鲁无礼的举动让在场所有人难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 churlish</td><td>/ˈtʃɜːrlɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁无礼的</td><td>a churlish refusal<br>无礼的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It would be churlish to refuse such a generous gift.  拒绝如此丰厚的礼物就太无礼了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clumsy</td><td>/ˈklʌmzi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不灵巧的</td><td>a clumsy attempt<br>笨拙的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My clumsy attempt at juggling ended with three broken plates.  我笨拙地学抛球，结果打碎了三个盘子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crude</td><td>/ˈkruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗糙的，未加工的；粗俗的</td><td>crude oil<br>原油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The furniture was crude but surprisingly sturdy and very cheap.  这家具做得粗糙，却意外结实又便宜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discourteous</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>a discourteous reply<br>无礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was discourteous to leave without thanking the host.  不向主人道谢就离开是无礼的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncouth</td><td>/ʌnˈkuːθ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无教养的</td><td>uncouth table manners<br>粗野的餐桌举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His uncouth remarks at dinner offended nearly everyone at the table.  他在晚餐上粗鲁的言辞几乎惹恼了同桌所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncivilized</td><td>/ʌnˈsɪvəlaɪzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未开化的，野蛮的</td><td>uncivilized behavior<br>野蛮的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Shouting at waiters is simply uncivilized behavior.  对服务员大喊大叫纯属野蛮行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncultured</td><td>/ʌnˈkʌltʃərd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有教养的，粗俗的</td><td>an uncultured remark<br>粗俗的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They dismissed him as uncultured because of his accent.  他们因他的口音就认定他没教养。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrefined</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗俗的，未提炼的</td><td>unrefined manners<br>粗俗的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unrefined manners embarrassed the whole table.  他粗俗的举止让整桌人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 courteous</td><td>/ˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的，谦恭有礼的</td><td>a courteous reply<br>礼貌的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even when customers shouted, the young clerk remained calm and courteous.  即使顾客大声嚷嚷，那位年轻店员仍然平静而有礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 civilized</td><td>/ˈsɪvəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>文明的，有教养的</td><td>civilized behavior<br>文明的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They settled the dispute in a calm, civilized manner over tea.  他们喝茶时以平静文明的方式解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 graceful</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>优雅的，优美的</td><td>a graceful dancer<br>姿态优雅的舞者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a graceful turn and landed without a sound.  她优雅地一个转身，落地时毫无声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polished</td><td>/ˈpɑːlɪʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的</td><td>polished manners<br>优雅得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polished manners impressed everyone at the formal dinner.  他优雅得体的举止让正式晚宴上的每个人都印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 refined</td><td>/rəˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精炼的，纯净的；有教养的</td><td>refined sugar<br>精制糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refined sugar has been stripped of every impurity.  精制糖已去除了所有杂质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 urbane</td><td>/ərˈbeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彬彬有礼的，文雅的</td><td>an urbane host<br>文雅的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our urbane host guided the conversation with easy wit and grace.  我们文雅的主人以轻松的机智和风度引导着谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lubricate</b></td><td>/ˈluːbrɪkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>润滑，涂润滑剂以减少摩擦</td><td>lubricate the moving parts<br>给活动部件上润滑油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Remember to lubricate the moving parts before you start the old engine.  启动这台旧发动机之前记得给活动部件上润滑油。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grease</td><td>/ˈɡriːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>给…涂油脂，润滑</td><td>grease the hinges<br>给合页上油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He greased the squeaking hinges with a drop of oil.  他滴了一点油润滑吱吱作响的合页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 oil</td><td>/ˈɔɪl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>给…加油，润滑</td><td>oil the chain<br>给链条上油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Remember to oil the bicycle chain every month.  记得每月给自行车链条上油。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slick</td><td>/ˈslɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使光滑；光滑的</td><td>slick back one's hair<br>把头发抹平贴后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He slicked back his hair before the interview.  面试前他把头发抹平贴后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 smooth</td><td>/ˈsmuːð/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>温和的，圆滑的；平滑的</td><td>a smooth talker<br>说话圆滑的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a smooth talker who never offends anyone in negotiations.  他谈判时言辞圆滑，从不得罪任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wax</td><td>/wæks/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>增大，增强</td><td>wax and wane<br>盛衰交替，此消彼长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence in the party waxed steadily during the long campaign.  在漫长的竞选中，他在党内的影响力稳步增强。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lucid</b></td><td>/ˈluːsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，易懂的</td><td>a lucid explanation<br>清晰的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor offered a lucid summary of a difficult argument.  教授对一个艰深论证做了清晰的总结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apprehensible</del></td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhensəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的</td><td>readily apprehensible ideas<br>易于理解的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument is barely apprehensible to readers without training.  他的论证对没有受过训练的读者几乎难以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comprehensible</td><td>/ˌkɑːmpriˈhensəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，易懂的</td><td>clear and comprehensible<br>清晰易懂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers make difficult ideas comprehensible to complete beginners.  好老师能让初学者也理解艰深的概念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intelligible</td><td>/ɪnˈtelɪdʒəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的</td><td>barely intelligible speech<br>几乎听不懂的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Speak slowly so your questions are intelligible to the audience.  慢点说，好让你的问题让听众听得懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palpable</td><td>/ˈpælpəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，易觉察的</td><td>a palpable sense of relief<br>明显的宽慰感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A palpable tension filled the room as the verdict was read aloud.  宣读判决时，房间里弥漫着明显的紧张气氛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patent</td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pellucid</td><td>/pəˈluːsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰明确的，易懂的</td><td>pellucid prose<br>明白易懂的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pellucid explanation made the theory seem obvious.  他清晰的讲解让这个理论显得浅显易懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 understandable</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈstændəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的；情有可原的</td><td>perfectly understandable<br>完全可以理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was understandable given how badly he was treated.  考虑到他受到的糟糕对待，他的愤怒可以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ambiguous</td><td>/æmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的，含义不明的</td><td>an ambiguous reply<br>含糊其辞的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's wording is ambiguous and invites future disputes.  合同措辞含义不明，容易引发日后争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enigmatic</td><td>/ˌenɪɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>神秘的，难以理解的</td><td>an enigmatic smile<br>神秘的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her enigmatic answer left the reporters guessing for several days.  她神秘的回答让记者们猜了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indistinct</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈstɪŋkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊不清的，含糊的</td><td>an indistinct outline<br>模糊的轮廓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photograph shows an indistinct figure by the door.  照片上门边有个模糊的人影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obfuscated</td><td>/ˈɑbfəskeɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混淆不清的，弄糊涂的</td><td>obfuscated legal language<br>晦涩的法律语言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report was so obfuscated that nobody could find the real numbers.  这份报告写得如此含混，没人能找到真实数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unclear</td><td>/ʌnˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不清楚的，不明确的</td><td>remain unclear<br>仍不明确</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is still unclear who made the final decision.  谁做的最终决定仍不清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lug</b></td><td>/lʌɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>费力搬运</td><td>lug a heavy suitcase<br>费力拖沉重的箱子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She lugged a heavy suitcase up four flights of narrow stairs.  她把一只沉重的箱子费力地拖上四层窄楼梯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bear</td><td>/ˈber/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>承受，支撑；忍受</td><td>bear the weight<br>承受重量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These thin beams cannot bear the weight of the roof.  这些细梁承受不了屋顶的重量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cart</td><td>/ˈkɑːrt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>用车运送；手推车</td><td>cart away the rubble<br>用车运走碎石</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They carted the bricks to the building site all morning.  他们整个上午都在用车把砖运到工地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convey</td><td>/kənˈveɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>运送，输送；传达</td><td>convey goods by rail<br>用铁路运送货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long pipeline conveys water from the mountains to the city.  一条长管道把水从山里输送到城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ferry</td><td>/ˈferi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>渡运，摆渡；渡船</td><td>ferry passengers across<br>把乘客渡到对岸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small boat ferries workers across the river each dawn.  每天黎明一艘小船把工人渡过河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haul</td><td>/ˈhɔːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>拖，拉；运送</td><td>haul logs to the mill<br>把原木拖到工厂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The truck hauled twenty tons of gravel up the hill.  卡车把二十吨砂石运上了山。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pack</td><td>/ˈpæk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>搬运，扛运；打包</td><td>pack supplies up the trail<br>把物资背上山路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guides packed our heavy gear up the steep trail.  向导把我们沉重的装备扛上陡峭的小路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tote</td><td>/ˈtoʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>手提，背负，携带</td><td>tote a bag of groceries<br>拎一袋日用杂货</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She toted her books across campus in an old canvas bag.  她用旧帆布袋拎着书穿过校园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transport</td><td>/trænsˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>运输，运送</td><td>transport the goods<br>运送货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trucks transport fresh produce to the city every morning.  卡车每天早晨把新鲜农产品运往城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lugubrious</b></td><td>/ləˈɡuːbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（故作夸张的）悲哀的</td><td>a lugubrious expression<br>夸张悲哀的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a lugubrious expression whenever anyone mentioned his ruined garden.  每次有人提到他毁掉的花园，他就露出夸张悲哀的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deploring</td><td>/dɪˈplɔːrɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>哀叹的，痛惜的</td><td>a deploring tone of voice<br>哀叹的语调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spoke of the ruined city in a deploring tone.  他用哀叹的语调谈起那座毁掉的城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doleful</td><td>/ˈdoʊlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲哀的，忧郁的</td><td>a doleful voice<br>悲哀的语调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She told the whole story in a low doleful voice.  她用低沉悲哀的语调讲完了整件事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dolorous</td><td>/ˈdoʊlərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧伤的</td><td>a dolorous sigh<br>忧伤的叹息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dolorous sigh escaped him whenever the subject of his hometown arose.  每次提到故乡，他都发出忧伤的叹息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lamentable</td><td>/ləˈmentəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惋惜的，可悲的</td><td>a lamentable failure<br>令人痛惜的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The condition of the old school building is truly lamentable.  这所旧校舍的状况实在令人惋惜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 melancholy</td><td>/ˈmelənkɑːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>忧郁，伤感</td><td>a mood of quiet melancholy<br>淡淡的忧郁情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Autumn rain always fills him with a vague melancholy.  秋雨总让他心里泛起一种莫名的忧郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 morose</td><td>/məˈroʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>grow morose and silent<br>变得忧郁沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing the election he grew morose and rarely left his apartment.  落选之后他变得忧郁，很少离开公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plaintive</td><td>/ˈpleɪntɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>哀伤的，悲切的</td><td>a plaintive melody<br>哀伤的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A plaintive melody drifted from the open window of the old house.  一段哀伤的旋律从那座老房子敞开的窗子里飘出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rueful</td><td>/ˈruːfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悔恨的，懊悔的</td><td>a rueful smile<br>懊悔的苦笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He admitted his error with a rueful smile and a shrug.  他带着懊悔的苦笑和一个耸肩承认了错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturnine</td><td>/ˈsætərnaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的，阴沉的</td><td>a saturnine temperament<br>阴沉的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His saturnine expression discouraged anyone from starting a cheerful conversation.  他阴沉的表情让人不敢开启轻松的话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sullen</td><td>/ˈsʌlən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷闷不乐的</td><td>a sullen silence<br>闷闷不乐的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sullen silence fell over the table when the topic was raised.  提到这个话题时，餐桌上陷入闷闷不乐的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 woeful</td><td>/ˈwoʊfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲惨的，糟糕的</td><td>a woeful performance<br>糟糕的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team's woeful defending cost them the championship again.  球队糟糕的防守又一次让他们丢了冠军。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delighted</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪtəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高兴的，欣喜的</td><td>delighted with the gift<br>对礼物很欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was delighted to hear about her friend's promotion.  听说朋友升职，她非常高兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 jocund</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɑːkənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢快的，高兴的</td><td>a jocund gathering<br>欢快的聚会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jocund crowd sang and danced until the last lantern went out.  欢快的人群唱歌跳舞，直到最后一盏灯笼熄灭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jovial</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊviəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愉快的</td><td>a jovial host<br>愉快的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our jovial host kept the guests laughing throughout the long winter evening.  我们愉快的主人让客人们在漫长的冬夜里笑声不断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lull</b></td><td>/lʌl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>相对平静时期，间隙；使镇静，使安心</td><td>a lull in the fighting<br>战斗中的间隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief lull in the fighting allowed medics to reach the wounded.  战斗中短暂的间隙让医护人员得以接近伤员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break</td><td>/ˈbreɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>损坏，打破；间歇</td><td>break the vase<br>打破花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cat broke the vase while chasing a moth indoors.  猫在屋里追蛾子时打破了花瓶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breath</td><td>/ˈbreθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一丝微风；呼吸</td><td>a breath of cool air<br>一丝凉爽的风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Not a breath of wind stirred the still lake at dusk.  黄昏时没有一丝风吹动平静的湖面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interruption</td><td>/ˌɪntəˈrʌpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>中断，打扰</td><td>without interruption<br>不受打扰地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She worked for three hours without a single interruption.  她连续工作三小时未被打扰一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recess</td><td>/rɪˈses/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>休会，休庭；课间休息</td><td>take a short recess<br>短暂休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee will recess for lunch and resume at two.  委员会将休会吃午饭，两点复会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balm</td><td>/bɑːm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>香油，止痛膏；安慰物</td><td>a balm for grief<br>抚慰悲伤之物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her quiet letters were a balm for his grief that winter.  那年冬天，她安静的信件是抚慰他悲伤之物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 becalm</del></td><td>/bɪˈkɑːm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，使停滞不动</td><td>becalmed by light winds<br>因风力太小而停滞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The yacht lay becalmed for two days without any breeze.  游艇因无风停滞了两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compose</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使镇定；组成；创作</td><td>compose oneself<br>让自己镇定下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a breath to compose himself before speaking.  他深吸一口气让自己镇定下来才开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lullaby</td><td>/ˈlʌləbaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>摇篮曲；唱摇篮曲使入睡</td><td>sing a gentle lullaby<br>唱一首轻柔的摇篮曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sang a gentle lullaby until the restless baby finally fell asleep.  她唱着轻柔的摇篮曲，直到不安的婴儿终于睡着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quiet</td><td>/ˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安静的；平静的</td><td>keep the room quiet<br>保持房间安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library stays quiet even during exam week.  即使在考试周，图书馆也保持安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salve</td><td>/sæv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，缓解</td><td>salve one's conscience<br>减轻内心的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave money to the school to salve his uneasy conscience.  他向学校捐钱，以减轻内心的不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 settle</td><td>/ˈsetəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解决，平息；定居</td><td>settle the dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally settled their dispute out of court.  双方最终在庭外解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 still</td><td>/ˈstɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>寂静无声的；寂静</td><td>the still of night<br>夜晚的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was still and the lake looked like glass.  空气静止无风，湖面像玻璃一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tranquilize</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使平静，给…服镇静剂</td><td>tranquilize the animal<br>给动物注射镇静剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vet had to tranquilize the frightened horse.  兽医不得不给这匹惊慌的马注射镇静剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agitate</td><td>/ˈædʒɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>煽动，激起；使不安</td><td>agitate for reform<br>鼓动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students agitated for changes to the exam system.  学生们为改革考试制度而奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 discompose</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安；使混乱</td><td>discompose the audience<br>让观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing seemed to discompose the veteran pilot, not even engine failure.  似乎没什么能让这位老飞行员心慌，连引擎故障也不行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disquiet</td><td>/dɪsˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>不安，忧虑；使不安</td><td>growing disquiet<br>日益增长的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is growing disquiet among parents about the new curriculum.  家长中对新课程的不安情绪日益增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disturb</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，扰乱；使不安</td><td>disturb the peace<br>扰乱安宁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please do not disturb the birds while they are nesting.  鸟类筑巢时请不要打扰它们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perturb</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrb/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使烦扰</td><td>perturb the balance<br>扰乱平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden change of plan perturbed everyone on the team.  计划的突然改变让全队的人都心神不宁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lullaby</b></td><td>/ˈlʌləbaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>摇篮曲；唱摇篮曲使入睡</td><td>sing a gentle lullaby<br>唱一首轻柔的摇篮曲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sang a gentle lullaby until the restless baby finally fell asleep.  她唱着轻柔的摇篮曲，直到不安的婴儿终于睡着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balm</td><td>/bɑːm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>香油，止痛膏；安慰物</td><td>a balm for grief<br>抚慰悲伤之物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her quiet letters were a balm for his grief that winter.  那年冬天，她安静的信件是抚慰他悲伤之物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 becalm</del></td><td>/bɪˈkɑːm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使平静，使停滞不动</td><td>becalmed by light winds<br>因风力太小而停滞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The yacht lay becalmed for two days without any breeze.  游艇因无风停滞了两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compose</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使镇定；组成；创作</td><td>compose oneself<br>让自己镇定下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a breath to compose himself before speaking.  他深吸一口气让自己镇定下来才开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lull</td><td>/lʌl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>相对平静时期，间隙；使镇静，使安心</td><td>a lull in the fighting<br>战斗中的间隙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief lull in the fighting allowed medics to reach the wounded.  战斗中短暂的间隙让医护人员得以接近伤员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quiet</td><td>/ˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>安静的；平静的</td><td>keep the room quiet<br>保持房间安静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library stays quiet even during exam week.  即使在考试周，图书馆也保持安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salve</td><td>/sæv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，缓解</td><td>salve one's conscience<br>减轻内心的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave money to the school to salve his uneasy conscience.  他向学校捐钱，以减轻内心的不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 settle</td><td>/ˈsetəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>解决，平息；定居</td><td>settle the dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally settled their dispute out of court.  双方最终在庭外解决了争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 still</td><td>/ˈstɪl/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>寂静无声的；寂静</td><td>the still of night<br>夜晚的寂静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The air was still and the lake looked like glass.  空气静止无风，湖面像玻璃一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tranquilize</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使平静，给…服镇静剂</td><td>tranquilize the animal<br>给动物注射镇静剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vet had to tranquilize the frightened horse.  兽医不得不给这匹惊慌的马注射镇静剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agitate</td><td>/ˈædʒɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>煽动，激起；使不安</td><td>agitate for reform<br>鼓动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students agitated for changes to the exam system.  学生们为改革考试制度而奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 discompose</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安；使混乱</td><td>discompose the audience<br>让观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing seemed to discompose the veteran pilot, not even engine failure.  似乎没什么能让这位老飞行员心慌，连引擎故障也不行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disquiet</td><td>/dɪsˈkwaɪət/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>不安，忧虑；使不安</td><td>growing disquiet<br>日益增长的不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is growing disquiet among parents about the new curriculum.  家长中对新课程的不安情绪日益增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disturb</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>打扰，扰乱；使不安</td><td>disturb the peace<br>扰乱安宁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please do not disturb the birds while they are nesting.  鸟类筑巢时请不要打扰它们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perturb</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrb/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使烦扰</td><td>perturb the balance<br>扰乱平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden change of plan perturbed everyone on the team.  计划的突然改变让全队的人都心神不宁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lumber</b></td><td>/ˈlʌmbər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>笨拙地行动；使负担（从而拖累）</td><td>lumber across the yard<br>笨拙地穿过院子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old bear lumbered across the yard toward the garbage cans.  那头老熊笨拙地穿过院子朝垃圾桶走去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flounder</td><td>/ˈflaʊndər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>笨拙地行动，挣扎</td><td>flounder in the water<br>在水中挣扎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The swimmer floundered in the cold water before help arrived.  救援到来之前，游泳者在冷水中挣扎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plod</td><td>/ˈplɑːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>沉重缓慢地走，苦干</td><td>plod through the mud<br>在泥地里沉重跋涉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tired horses plodded through the deep mud.  疲惫的马匹在深泥中沉重跋涉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stumble</td><td>/ˈstʌmbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>绊倒，跌跌撞撞</td><td>stumble over a root<br>被树根绊倒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stumbled on the dark staircase but did not fall.  她在漆黑的楼梯上绊了一下，但没有摔倒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trudge</td><td>/trʌdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>吃力而笨拙地走</td><td>trudge through the snow<br>在雪中吃力行走</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We trudged home through deep snow with our heavy canvas bags.  我们背着沉重的帆布包，在深雪中吃力地走回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burden</td><td>/ˈbɜːrdən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使负重担；负担</td><td>burden him with debt<br>让他背上债务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They burdened the young manager with impossible sales targets.  他们让这位年轻经理背上无法完成的销售指标。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encumber</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freight</td><td>/ˈfreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>装货于；货运</td><td>freight a ship with grain<br>给船装满谷物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The barge was freighted with coal for the northern mills.  驳船装满了运往北方工厂的煤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lade</td><td>/ˈleɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>装载，装货</td><td>lade a cart with hay<br>给车装满干草</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers laded the wagon with heavy sacks of flour.  工人们把一袋袋沉重的面粉装上马车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laden</td><td>/ˈleɪdən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>满载的，负重的</td><td>laden with heavy bags<br>拎满沉重的袋子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She arrived laden with gifts for the whole family.  她抱着给全家的礼物来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saddle</td><td>/ˈsædl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使某人负担</td><td>saddle him with blame<br>让他背上责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rules saddle small farmers with paperwork they cannot possibly manage.  新规让小农户背上他们根本应付不了的文书负担。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weight</td><td>/ˈweɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>重要性，影响力；重量</td><td>carry great weight<br>极有影响力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her opinion carries considerable weight with the committee.  她的意见在委员会里很有分量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glide</td><td>/ɡlaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>轻松地行动，轻松地通过</td><td>glide across the floor<br>轻盈地滑过地板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The skaters glided across the frozen pond without making a single sound.  滑冰者们悄无声息地轻盈滑过结冰的池塘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slide</td><td>/ˈslaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>滑行，顺畅移动；下滑</td><td>slide into the room<br>悄然滑进房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She slid into the room without making any sound.  她悄无声息地滑进了房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disburden</del></td><td>/dɪsˈbɜːrdən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>卸下负担；解除烦恼</td><td>disburden oneself of worries<br>卸下心中的忧虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He finally disburdened himself of the secret he had kept for years.  他终于卸下了多年来藏着的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discharge</td><td>/dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放；解雇</td><td>discharge the prisoner<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court discharged the prisoner immediately after the new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，法庭立即释放了那名囚犯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disencumber</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使摆脱累赘，卸去负担</td><td>disencumber a ship of cargo<br>卸掉船上的货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She disencumbered herself of the heavy coat and sat down.  她脱掉厚重的外套坐了下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unlade</del></td><td>/ʌnˈleɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>卸货，卸下</td><td>unlade the cargo hold<br>卸空货舱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dockers began to unlade the freighter at first light.  码头工人天一亮就开始给货轮卸货。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unload</td><td>/ʌnˈloʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>卸货，卸下；倾诉</td><td>unload boxes from the van<br>从货车上卸下箱子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They unloaded all the furniture before the rain started.  他们在下雨前把家具都卸完了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>luminary</b></td><td>/ˈluːmɪneri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>杰出人物</td><td>a luminary in physics<br>物理学界的名家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several luminaries in physics gathered to honor their retiring old professor.  物理学界的几位名人聚在一起向他们退休的老教授致敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 celebrity</td><td>/səˈlebrɪti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>名人；名声</td><td>a local television celebrity<br>当地的电视名人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The restaurant is full of celebrities every Friday night.  每周五晚这家餐厅都挤满名人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eminence</td><td>/ˈemənəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>卓越，杰出；显赫人物</td><td>rise to eminence<br>崭露头角，声名显赫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He achieved eminence as a surgeon before he turned forty.  他四十岁前就成了声名卓著的外科医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 figure</td><td>/ˈfɪɡjər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>身材，身影；数字；人物</td><td>a slender figure<br>苗条的身材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A tall figure appeared suddenly at the end of the corridor.  一个高大的身影突然出现在走廊尽头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 icon</td><td>/ˈaɪkɑn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>偶像，标志性人物；圣像</td><td>a cultural icon<br>文化偶像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became an icon for a whole generation of young scientists.  她成了整整一代年轻科学家的偶像。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 notability</td><td>/ˌnoʊtəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>名人，要人；显著</td><td>a local notability<br>本地的名流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several notabilities attended the opening of the new library.  几位名流出席了新图书馆的开馆仪式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 star</td><td>/ˈstɑːr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>明星，名角；星</td><td>the star of the show<br>演出的头号明星</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was the star of every school play she joined.  她参演的每部校园剧里她都是主角。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superstar</td><td>/ˈsuːpərstɑːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>超级明星，巨星</td><td>an international superstar<br>国际巨星</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pitcher became a superstar in a single season.  这位年轻投手一个赛季就成了超级明星。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nobody</td><td>/ˈnoʊˌbɑːdi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>无名小卒，小人物</td><td>treated like a nobody<br>被当作无名小卒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in the city a nobody and left it famous.  他到这座城市时无人知晓，离开时已成名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nonentity</td><td>/nɑːˈnentəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不重要的人</td><td>a political nonentity<br>政坛上的无名之辈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Once a political nonentity, he suddenly became the party's most quoted voice.  他曾是政坛的无名之辈，突然成了党内被引用最多的声音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lurch</b></td><td>/lɜːrtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>蹒跚</td><td>lurch to one side<br>向一侧猛地歪倒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bus lurched to one side and threw passengers against the windows.  公交车猛地向一侧歪倒，把乘客甩到窗户上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careen</td><td>/kəˈriːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇晃疾行，横冲直撞</td><td>careen down the hill<br>摇晃着冲下山坡</td></tr>
