<tr><td>↳反 sedentary</td><td>/ˈsednteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固定不动的</td><td>a sedentary lifestyle<br>久坐不动的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of sedentary office work had weakened his back and shoulders.  多年久坐不动的办公工作削弱了他的背和肩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>milk</b></td><td>/mɪlk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>榨取（财富、信息等）</td><td>milk a source for information<br>从线人处榨取信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters tried to milk the official for details he was not allowed to share.  记者试图从这位官员那里榨取他无权透露的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drain</td><td>/ˈdreɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>耗尽，使枯竭</td><td>drain the country's resources<br>耗尽国家资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long war drained the country's resources and its morale.  漫长的战争耗尽了国家的资源和士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exploit</td><td>/ɪkˈsplɔɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>最大程度地利用</td><td>exploit natural resources<br>开发自然资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to exploit the island's natural resources responsibly.  公司计划负责任地开发这个岛的自然资源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mime</b></td><td>/maɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>模仿</td><td>mime the act of drinking<br>模仿喝水的动作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actor mimed the act of drinking tea so convincingly that everyone laughed.  演员模仿喝茶的动作逼真到让所有人都笑了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ape</td><td>/ˈeɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>模仿，学样；猿</td><td>ape someone's accent<br>模仿某人的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy aped his father's walk to make everyone laugh.  男孩模仿父亲走路的样子逗大家笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copy</td><td>/ˈkɑːpi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品，仿制品；抄写</td><td>a cheap copy<br>廉价仿制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting on the wall is only a cheap copy.  墙上那幅画只是廉价的仿制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copycat</td><td>/ˈkɑːpiˌkæt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>盲目模仿者，抄袭者</td><td>a copycat crime<br>模仿性的犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine was a shameless copycat of a French title.  这本杂志无耻地照抄了一份法国刊物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emulate</td><td>/ˈemjuleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>效仿</td><td>emulate his father's style<br>效仿他父亲的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young painter tried to emulate his father's style in every canvas.  这位年轻画家在每幅画布上都力图效仿他父亲的风格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mimic</td><td>/ˈmɪmɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>模仿，仿效；仿造的</td><td>mimic human speech<br>模仿人类说话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Certain parrots can mimic human speech well enough to fool a casual listener.  某些鹦鹉能模仿人类说话，足以骗过不留意的听者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simulate</td><td>/ˈsɪmjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>模拟，假装</td><td>simulate battle conditions<br>模拟作战环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The training program can simulate battle conditions inside a quiet classroom.  该训练项目能在安静的教室里模拟作战环境。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mimic</b></td><td>/ˈmɪmɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>模仿，仿效；仿造的</td><td>mimic human speech<br>模仿人类说话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Certain parrots can mimic human speech well enough to fool a casual listener.  某些鹦鹉能模仿人类说话，足以骗过不留意的听者。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imitative</td><td>/ˈɪməˌteɪtɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模仿的，仿造的</td><td>imitative behavior<br>模仿性的行为</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sent a minatory letter demanding payment within one week.  他寄来一封带威胁的信，要求一周内付款。</td></tr>
</tbody>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 His baleful influence on the young players worried the coach.  他对年轻球员的有害影响让教练担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 direful</del></td><td>/ˈdaɪrfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，凄惨的</td><td>direful warnings<br>可怕的警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old prophet issued direful warnings about the coming winter.  老先知对将至的冬天发出可怕的警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doomy</td><td>/ˈduːmi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>凶兆的，阴郁悲观的</td><td>a doomy prediction<br>不祥的预言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Newspapers printed doomy forecasts about the country's economy.  报纸刊出了对国家经济的悲观预测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreboding</td><td>/fɔːrˈboʊdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不祥的；不祥的预感</td><td>a sense of foreboding<br>不祥的预感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt a strange foreboding as the sky darkened.  天色转暗时她有种奇怪的不祥预感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ill-boding</td><td>/ɪl ˈboʊdɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>预示凶兆的，不祥的</td><td>ill-boding clouds<br>预示不祥的云</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ill-boding clouds gathered over the harbor before noon.  中午前不祥的云聚集在港口上空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inauspicious</td><td>/ˌɪnɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不吉利的，不祥的</td><td>an inauspicious start<br>不吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made an inauspicious start by losing twice at home.  球队主场连输两场，开局不利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 menacing</td><td>/ˈmenəsɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>威胁的，凶险的</td><td>a menacing look<br>凶险的眼神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two menacing dogs stood at the end of the narrow lane.  两条凶恶的狗站在窄巷尽头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portentous</td><td>/pɔːrˈtentəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>预兆性的，凶兆的</td><td>a portentous silence<br>预示不祥的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A portentous silence fell over the room before the verdict was read.  宣读判决之前，房间里陷入一阵预示不祥的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sinister</td><td>/ˈsɪnɪstər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴险的，不祥的</td><td>a sinister smile<br>阴险的笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was something sinister about the empty old house.  那座空荡的老房子有种阴森之感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 threatening</td><td>/ˈθretənɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>威胁的；（天气）要变坏的</td><td>a threatening gesture<br>威胁性的手势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made a threatening gesture and then walked quickly away.  他做了个威胁的手势，然后快步走开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unthreatening</td><td>/ˌʌnˈθrɛtənɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不具威胁的，温和的</td><td>an unthreatening manner<br>毫无威胁感的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unthreatening manner made the children relax at once.  她毫无威胁感的态度让孩子们立刻放松了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ministration</b></td><td>/ˌmɪnɪˈstreɪʃn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>照料；服侍；牧师职务</td><td>the ministrations of a nurse<br>护士的照护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient recovered quickly under the gentle ministrations of a nurse.  在护士温柔的照料下，病人很快康复了。</td></tr>
